NOTICE 
New Delhi , the 21st March. 1990 
S . O . 898. - Notice is hereby given by the Competent 
Authority in pursuance of rule 6 of the Notaries , 1956 , 
thut application has been made to the said Authority 
under rule 4 of the said Rules , by Shri Sat Paul Singh 
Sodhi. Advocate for appointment as a Notary to practise 
in Julsndhar ( Pb. ), 


नई दिल्ली, 21 मार्च , 1990 


का . प्रा . 808. - अनोटरीज नियम , 1956 के नियम 6 
के अनुसरण में मदम प्राधिकारी द्वारा यह सूचना दी जाती 
है कि श्री मतपाल सिंह सोढ़ी एडवोकेट ने उक्त प्राधिकारी 
को उक्त नियम के नियम 4 के अधीन एक भावेदन इम 
बात के लिए दिया है कि उसे जालन्धर , पंजाब में व्यवसाय 
करने के लिये नोटरी के संप में नियक्ति पर कि भी 
प्रकार का आपेक्ष इस सूचना के प्रकाशन के चौदह दिन 
के भीतर लिखित स.प में मेरे पास भेजा जाए । 


Any obiection to the appointment of the said person 
as a Notary may be submitted in writing to the undersigacd 
within fourieen days of the publication of this Notice. 

[ No. F. 5(12)/ 90-Judl.]

S. O . 908.-.-It is hereby notified for general information 
that the organisation mentioned below has been approved by 
the Prescribed Authority under Rule 6 of the Income-tax 
Rules, 1962 , i. e , the Director General (I. T . Exemptions ) in 
concurrence with the Secretary, Department of Scientific & 
Tndustrial Research, for the purposes of clause (ii ) of sub 
section 35 ( Thirty Five / one /two ) of the Income tax Act , 
1961 under the category "Institution " subject to the follow 
ing conditions : 
(i) The organisation will maintain a separate account 

of the sums received by it for Scientific Research . 
(ii) It will furnish the Annual Return of its scientific 

roscarch activities to the Secretary , Department of 
Scientific & Industrial Research, Technology 
Bhavan, New Mehrauli Road , New Delhi-110016 

for every financial year by 31st May of each year . 
(iii ) It will submit to the ( a ) Director General (Income 

tax Fremrntions ), ( o ) Secretary , Department of 
Scientific & Industrial Research, and ( c ) Commis 
sioner of Income-tax / Director of Income -tax 
( Exemptions ) having jurisdiction over the organisa 
tion, by the 30th June each year n copy of its audited

संगठन को , आयकर अधिनियम 1961 की धारा 35 (पैतीस / 
एक / दो ) की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खंड ( ii ) के लिए, सचिव ,

कारी अर्थात् महानिदेशक ( आयकर छूट ) द्वारा निम्नलिखिन 
शती पर संघ " प्रवर्ग के अधीन अनुमोदित किया गया है : 
(i ) संगठन वैज्ञानिक अनुसंधान के लिये प्राप्त धन के 

लिये एक अलग लेखा रखेगा । 
(ii ) यह अपने वज्ञानिक अनुसंधान संबंधी कार्यकलापों का 

एक वाषिक विवरण प्रत्येक वित्तीय वर्ष के लिये , 
प्रत्येक वर्ष की 31 मई तक , सचिव, वैज्ञानिक व 

औद्योगिक अनुसंधान विभाग , औधोगिक भवन , 

न्यू मेहरौली रोड, नई दिल्ली -110016 को भेजेगा । 
( iii ) यह प्रत्येक वर्ष की 30 जून तक , लेखा-परीक्षित 

वार्षिक लेखों की एक प्रति अपनी प्राय -व्यय , 
प्रास्तियों एवं देनवारियों के विवरण सहित ( क ) 
महानिदेशक ( आयकर छूट ) ( ख ) सचिव वैज्ञानिक व 
औद्योगिक अनुसंधान विभाग , और ( ग ) आयकर 
श्रायुक्त/ आयकर निदेशक ( छूट ), जिनके क्षेत्रा 

धिकार में पड़ता है , को प्रस्तुत करेगा । 
( iv ) अनुमोदन की अवधि बढ़ाने के लिये, यह अनुमोदन 

की समाप्ति के तीन माह पूर्व आयकर आयुक्त

S . O . 941 . --- It is hereby notified ofr general information 
that the organisation mentioned below has been approved by 
the Prescribed Authority under Rule 6 of the Income-tax 
Rules , 1962 , i. e., the Director General (I , T . Exemptions) 
in concurrence with the Secretary , Department of Scientific 
and Industrial Research , for the purposes of clauko (ii) of 
sub -section ( 1 ) of Section 35 ( Thirty Five /one /two ) of the 
Income -tax Act, 1961 under the category " Association " sub 
ject to the following conditions ; 


( i) The organisation will maintain a separate account of 

the sumg received by it for Scientific Research , 


( ii) It will furnish thc Annual Return of its scientific 

research activities to the Secretary , Department of 
Scientific and Industrial Research , Technology 
Bhavan , Now Mchrauli Road , New Delhi- 110016 

for every financial year by 31st May of each year. 
( iii) It will subroit to the ( a ) Director General (Income 

tax Exemptions ), ( b ) Secretary, Department of Sci 
entific and Industrial Research and (c ) Commissinoer 
of Income -tax / Director of Income -tax ( Exemptions ) 
having jurisdiction over the organisation, by tho 30th 
June each year a copy of its audited annual 
Accounts showing its income and expenditure , and 
its assets and liabilites.

का . आ . 941 --- सर्वसाधारण की सूचना के लिए 
एतदद्वारा यह अधिसुचना किया जाता है कि निम्नलिखित 
संगठन को , ग्रायकर अधिनियम 1961 की धारा 35 (पेंतीस 

एक दो ) की उपधाग ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( ii ) के लिये , सचिव , 
वैज्ञानिक और औद्योगिक अनसंधान विभाग की सहमति से , 
प्रायकर नियम 1962 के नियम G के अधीन विहित प्राधि 
कारी प्रार्थात महानिदेशक ( प्रायकर छट ) द्वारा निम्नलिखित 
शों पर “ संघ ” प्रवर्ग के अधीन अनुमोदित किया गया है । 
( i ) मंगटन वैज्ञानिक अनुसंधान के लिये प्राप्त धन 

के लिये एक अलग लेखा रग्नेगा । 
यह अपने बैज्ञानिक अनुसंधन संबंधी कार्यकलापों . 
का एक वार्षिक विवरण प्रत्येक वित्तीय वर्ष के 
लिये , प्रत्येक वर्ष की 31 मई तक , सचिः , वैज्ञा 
निक व औद्योगिक अनसंधान विभाग , औद्योगिक 
भवन , त्य मेहरौली रोड, नई दिल्ली- 110016 

को भेजेगा । 
( iii ) यह प्रत्येक वर्ष की 30 जून तक , लेखा-परीक्षित 

वार्षिक लेखों की एक प्रति अपनी प्राय-व्यय , 
ग्रास्तियों एवं देनदारियों के विवरण सहित , ( क ) 
महानिदेशक ( प्राथकर एट ) ( ख ) मचित्र , वैज्ञानिक 
व औद्योगिक अनुसंधान विभाग, और ( ग ) आयकर 
पायुक्त नायकर निदेशक ( छूट ) , जिनके क्षेत्रा 
धिकार में पड़ता है , को प्रस्तुत करेगा । 
अनुमोदन की अवधि बढ़ाने के लिये , यह अनुमोदन 
की ममाप्ति के तीन माह पूर्व प्रायकर यायुक्त 

आयकर निदेशक ( छुट ) , जिनके क्षेत्राधिकार में 
संगठन पड़ता है, के माध्यम से महानिदेशक 
( प्रायकर छूट ) कलकत्ता को तीन प्रतियों में 
आवेदन प्रस्तुत करेगा । यह अनुमोदन की अवधि 
बढ़ाने के संबंध में किये गये आवेदन-पत्र की 
6 प्रतिया मचिन, वैज्ञानिक और औद्योगिक अन 
मंधान विभाग, को भी प्रस्तुत करेगा । 


( iv ) It will apply in triplicate for further extension of tho 

Approval, to the Director General (Income-tax Ex 
emptions), Calcutta through the Commissioner of 
Income-tax/ the Director of Income-tax ( Exemptions ) 
having jurisdiction over the organisation , three 
months before the expiry of the approval. It will 
also submit six copies of the application for exten 
sion to the Secretary , Department of Scientific and 
Industrial Research.

S. 0 . 947. --- It is hereby notified for general information 
that the organisation mentioned below has been approved by 
the Prescribed Authority under Rule 6 of the Income-tax 
Rules , 1962, i. e., the Director General ( I . T . Exemptions ) 
in concurrence with the Secretary , Department of Scientific 
and Industrial Research , for the purposes of clause (ii ) of 
sub -section ( 1 ) of Section 35 ( Thirty Five / one / two ) of the 
Income -tax Act , 1961 under the category " Association " sub 
ject to the following conditions : 
( i) The organisation will maintain a separate account of 

the sums received by it for Scientific Research . 
( ii) It will furnish the Annual Return of its scientific 

research activties to the Secretary , Department of 
Scientific and Industrial Research , Technology 
Bhavan , New Mehrauli Road , New Delhi- 110016 

for every financial year by 31st May of each year . 
(iii) It will submit to the ( a ) Director General (Income 

tax Exemptions ), ( b ) Secretary , Department of Sci 
entific and Indusrtial Research and ( c ) Commissioner 
of Income -tax / Director of Income- tax ( Exemptions ) 
having jurisdiction over the organisation , by the 
30th June each year a copy of its audited annual 
accounts showing its income and expenditure , and 

its assets and liabilities . 
(iv ) It will apply in triplicate for further extension of the 

approval, to the Director General (Income-tax Ex 
emptions), Calcutta through the Commissioner of 
Income-tax / the Director of Income- tax (Exemptions) 
having jurisdiction over the organisation , three 
months before the expiry of the approval. It will 
also submit six copies of the application for exten 
sion to the Secretary , Department of Scientific and 
Industrial Research .

( पैंतीस /एक / दो ) की उप धारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( ii ) के लिए ,

financial year by 31st May of each year.

का . प्रा . 1002: - - केन्द्रीय सरकार ने , कोयला धारक क्षेत्र ( अर्जन और विकास ) अधिनियम , 1957 ( 1957 
का 10 ) की धारा 7 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत के राजपत्र , भाग 2, खंड 3, उपखंड ( 2 ) तारीख 2 अप्रैल , 1988 में 
प्रकाशित भारत सरकार के ऊर्जा मंत्रालय ( कोयला विभाग ) की अधिसूचना सं . का , प्रा . 999, तारीख 3 मार्च, 1988 
द्वारा उस अधिस चना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट परिक्षत्र में भूमि और अधिकारों के अर्जन के अपने प्राशय की सूचना 
दी थी ; 

और अब सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 8 के अनुसरण में केन्द्रीय सरकार को अपनी रिपोर्ट दी 


और सब केन्द्रीय सरकार का उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात् और उड़ीसा सरकार से परामर्ण करने के पश्चात् 
समाधान हो गया है कि इसमें संलग्न अनुसूची में वर्णित 1898 , 964 एकष्ट ( लगभग ) या 768 . 500 हैक्टर ( लगभग ) भूमि 
फा वर्जन कर लिया जाना चाहिए । 
अतः, अब केन्द्रीय सरकार , कोयला धारक क्षेस ( अर्जन और विकास ) अधिनियम , 1957 ( 1957 का 20 ) की 

द्वारा प्रदन शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए यह घोषणा करती है कि उक्त अनुगूची में वर्णित 
1898 , 96 एकर ( लगभग ) या 768 . 500 हेक्टर ( लगभग ) भूमि का अर्जन कर लिया गया है ।

in the locality specified in the Schedule appended to that notification . 

And whereas the Competent Authority in pursuance of section 8 of the said Act has made his report to the 
Centrul Government. 


And whereas the, Central Government after considering the report aforesaid and after consulting the Govern 
inent of Orissa is satisfied that the land nieasuring 1898 . 964 acres ( upproximately ) or 768 .500 hectares (approxi 
matcly ) described in the Schedule aprended hercto should bc acquired . 


Now , thereforc in cxcrcise of the powers copferred by sub - section (i) of section 9 of the Coal Bearing Areas 
(Acquisition and Developnicnt) Act, 1957 ( 20 of 1957) the Central Government hereby declares that the land mea 
suring 1898 . 964 acres (approximately ) or 768 . 500 hectares (approximately ) described in the said schedule are 
hereby acquired.

MINISTRY OF LABOUR 

Now Delhi, the 20th March , 1990 
S . O . 1007. - In pursuance of Section 17 of the Industrial 
Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the Central Government 
hereby published the following award of the Central Govin 
mont Indusstial Tribunal No. 1 Dhanbad as shown in the 
Annexure in the industrial dispute between the employer , in 
relation to the management of State Bank of India and their 
workmen which was received by the Central Government 
on 19 - 3 - 1990. . . 


The management has stated in the written statement that 
the present reference is not maintainable as it has been mude 
without any application of mind and that there is nothing 
to be adjudicated upon because no legal inquiry was causeil , 
The substantive case of the management is that State Ban 
of India is a body corporate constituted under the provisio , 
of the State Bank of India Act, 1955 1 : 1 dities , M : Wers . 
functions and exponsibilitiny of he officials of th : 
Bunk are regula er by the provisi: Ds of St., Binh on Indit . 
Act, 1955 and also by regulations and rules mude thereunder 
and also by circular issued by the Bank from time to timo. 
In terms of the said Act, Rules and Regulations the Bank 
issued circular from time to time laying down rules, modes , 
eligibility criteria etc . Ono such circular No. Per /84 of 
1969 which was applicable at the relevant time prescribed 
the eligibility criteria for appointment of suhordinate staff , 
the details of which are as under ; 
( A ) Age Minimum 18 yours 

Maximum 24 years 
(b ) Academic qualification-- not passed matriculation 

examination , 
The concerned workman was engaged when he did not fulfil 
the above eligiblev criteria as he was over aged Aud over 
aged and over qualified which rendered his appointment void 
aliriio and illegal "The concerned workmi? Corinced 10 
Work iu Ghatotal Branch as a subordinate staff till 4 - 4 -87 
Vien ? ! gajendrat ay messelaar was terminated tici it 
was noticed and discovered by the Controlling Authorities 
of the Branch Manager of the aforesaid Branch that very 
appointment of the concerned workman was void abinitio , 
irregular and unauthorised . The concerned workman was 
never uppointed against a permanent vacancy and was en 
paged on purely temporary basis. Both his age and quali 
fication were inconsistent with and contrary to the specifica 
tions as laid down in the circular of the Bank as aforexiid , 
Since zippointment of the concerned workman Was 
void abinitiof it could not confer upon liim any right of 
workman or employee nor can he come within the purview 
of dſiniton of workman as crvisaged in Industrial Disputes 
Art. Anyway, in order to avoid any complication as also 

relying on the case reported is AIR 1976 $ C . 1111. the 
Bank has been generous to pay him the retrenchment com 
pensation in compassionate ground and as special caso and 
algo as a measure of abundant precaution though he was not 
entitled to retrenchment compensation . He is painfully cm 
ployed elsewhere after termination of his service with effect 
from 4 - 4 -87 . It has been submitted that if such type of 
appointment which was void abinițio is encouraged it would 
open Mond gate far corrupt practices in the maller of appoint 
ment.

New Delhi, the 21st March , 1990 
S . O . 1008. — In pursuance of Section 17 of the Industrial 
Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ), the Central Government 
hereby publishes the award of the Industriul Tribunal, 
ilyderabad as shown in the Annexulc in the industrial disputo 
between the employers in iclation to the management of 
Viakhapatnam Port Trust, Visakhapatnam and their work 
men , which was received by the Central Govenrment on 
20 - 3 - 1990 . 


15 . The management of the Bank has produced documents 
Ext. M - 1 to show that maximum age limit for recriutment 
OY subordinato staff was / is 24 years . But document pro 
duced by the sponsoring union indicates that the upper age 
limit wus relaxed to 26 years ( Ext, W - 7 ). 1 his document 
and other documents , such as , Ext. Way , W - 5 , W -6 and 
W - 8 do not indicate that appointment of a subordinate staff 
who has crossed tho Lipper age limit is illegal. The educa 
tional qualification for appointincnt of subordinate staff is 
8th Class Passed but not 10th Class Passed . But iho docu 
ments do not indicate that the appointment of over -qualified 
subordinate staff is illegal. Admittedly , the concerned work 
man has passed School Final Examination equivalent to matri 
culation . In view of my discussion above I hold that the 
appointment of the concerned workman as a subordinato 
staff was not illegal simply because he was over- aged or 
Over - qualified ,

APPEARANCES : 


16 . Admittedly , the management of the Bank did not 
pay annual incroment to the concerned workman . The Bank 
has taken the plea that tho concerned worlman was not 
entitled to annual increment as he was not cntitled therefor . 
But cven as per the case of the management of tho 
Bank thc alleged over - qualification and over- age of the con 
cerned workman was detected by the Controlling Authority 
of the Branch Manager at a later stage and when it was 
$ 0 detected his services werc terminated . The concerned 
workman was appointed on a temporary bas19 on 1 - 1 - 1982 
and continued to work as temporary basis till 4 - 4 - 1987. If 
the over - qualification or over -age of the concerned workman 
was detected by the management of the Bank at a later stage 
than withholding of annual increment will also occur at a 
later stage and not after one year of completion of service. 
a temporary cmployee is entitled to annual increment for 
cach period of 12 months service (Ext. W - 2 ) . It appears 
that the concerned workman was entitled to annual incre 
ment on 1 - 1 -83, 1- 1 - 84 , 1 - 1 - 83 and 1 - 1 - 86 . But the Bank 
did not pay all these increments to him . This can not but 
be considered as an infair labour practice on the rart of 
the Bank . However, considering all these facts anel circuins 
tances and evidence on record I am constrained to hold 
that the action of the Branch Manager of Ghatotard Branch 
of State Bank of India in terminating the servicc of the con 
cerned workman by order dated 4 - 4 - 87 is not justified . The 
concerned workmin is entitled to be reinstated in service 
with effect from 5 - 4 - 87 with full back wages. He is also 
entitled to annual increment as per rules.
